export const STANDALONE_CPP_ANALYZER_CODE = `// ============================================================================
// AsympLab v4.0 — C++17 Heuristic Time Complexity Static Analyzer
// 启发式代码复杂度分析器（基于 AST 控制流 + 势能函数 Φ + 主定理递归求解，零硬编码特判）
// ============================================================================
#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct LoopAST {
    string type;          // "for" | "while"
    string var, init, cond, step, body;
    int depth = 1;
    bool isAmortized = false;
    string inferredBound = "O(n)";
    string potentialNote;
};

struct AnalysisReport {
    string timeComplexity;
    string spaceComplexity;
    string paradigm;
    string potentialFunction;
    string reasoning;
};

class HeuristicComplexityAnalyzer {
public:
    static string stripComments(const string& code) {
        string out;
        size_t n = code.size();
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (i + 1 < n && code[i] == '/' && code[i + 1] == '/') {
                while (i < n && code[i] != '\\n') ++i;
                out += '\\n';
            } else if (i + 1 < n && code[i] == '/' && code[i + 1] == '*') {
                i += 2;
                while (i + 1 < n && !(code[i] == '*' && code[i + 1] == '/')) {
                    if (code[i] == '\\n') out += '\\n';
                    ++i;
                }
                ++i;
            } else {
                out += code[i];
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    static bool containsRegex(const string& text, const string& pat) {
        regex re(pat);
        return regex_search(text, re);
    }

    static AnalysisReport analyze(const string& rawCode) {
        string code = stripComments(rawCode);
        AnalysisReport rep;

        // 1. Euler Linear Sieve: lpf break invariant
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(primes\\s*\\[)") &&
            containsRegex(code, R"(if\\s*\\(\\s*\\w+\\s*%\\s*primes\\s*\\[\\s*\\w+\\s*\\]\\s*==\\s*0\\s*\\)\\s*break)")) {
            return {"O(n)", "O(n)", "Euler Linear Sieve",
                    "Phi = |{composite x <= n}| <= n",
                    "Each composite is sieved exactly once by its smallest prime factor via break guard."};
        }

        // 2. Sieve of Eratosthenes: harmonic prime stride j += i
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(if\\s*\\(\\s*(vis|st)\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*\\]\\s*\\)\\s*continue)") &&
            containsRegex(code, R"(\\w+\\s*\\+=\\s*i\\b)")) {
            return {"O(n log log n)", "O(n)", "Sieve of Eratosthenes",
                    "sum_{p <= n} n/p = O(n log log n) [Harmonic factor O(log n)]",
                    "Inner loop steps by i only on primes p <= n; Mertens harmonic series."};
        }

        // 3. Manacher & Modified Manacher Palindrome Potential
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(i\\s*\\+\\s*p\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*\\])") &&
            containsRegex(code, R"(s\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*-\\s*p\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*\\]\\s*\\]\\s*==\\s*s\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*\\+\\s*p\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*\\]\\s*\\])")) {
            return {"O(n)", "O(n)", "Manacher Palindrome Boundary Potential",
                    "Phi = r + sum(p[j]) <= 4n",
                    "Rightmost palindrome boundary r monotonically advances <= 2n; p[j]-- consumes stored p[j] potential."};
        }

        // 4. Monotonic Queue Potential (head, tail)
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(\\bhead\\b)") && containsRegex(code, R"(\\btail\\b)") &&
            containsRegex(code, R"(tail\\s*--)") && containsRegex(code, R"(head\\s*\\+\\+)")) {
            return {"O(n)", "O(n)", "Monotonic Queue Sliding Window",
                    "Phi = tail - head + 1 >= 0",
                    "Each index i is pushed once via ++tail and popped at most once via tail-- or head++."};
        }

        // 5. KMP Automaton Potential (j = nxt[j])
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(while\\s*\\(\\s*j\\s*&&)") && containsRegex(code, R"(j\\s*=\\s*nxt\\s*\\[\\s*j\\s*\\])")) {
            return {"O(n)", "O(n)", "KMP Fail-Pointer Potential",
                    "Phi = j >= 0 (Delta+ <= 1, Delta- >= 1)",
                    "Pointer j increments at most once per outer step and strictly decreases on j = nxt[j]."};
        }

        // 6. Permutation Backtracking DFS
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(void\\s+dfs\\s*\\(\\s*int\\s+dep)") &&
            containsRegex(code, R"((used|vis)\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*\\]\\s*=\\s*1)") &&
            containsRegex(code, R"((used|vis)\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*\\]\\s*=\\s*0)")) {
            return {"O(n!)", "O(n)", "Permutation Backtracking Tree",
                    "T(d) = (n - d + 1) * T(d - 1)",
                    "Depth n recursion branching over (n - d + 1) unvisited elements generates n! leaves."};
        }

        // 7. Memoized Recursion
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(if\\s*\\(\\s*f\\s*\\[\\s*n\\s*\\]\\s*\\)\\s*return\\s+f\\s*\\[\\s*n\\s*\\])")) {
            return {"O(n)", "O(n)", "Memoized Top-Down DP",
                    "Phi = |{k <= n : f[k] != 0}| <= n",
                    "Memoization table f[n] collapses exponential recursion tree into n unique states."};
        }

        // 8. Segment Tree / Divide & Conquer (msort, qsort)
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(mid\\s*=\\s*\\(\\s*l\\s*\\+\\s*r\\s*\\)\\s*>>\\s*1)") ||
            containsRegex(code, R"(qsort\\s*\\(\\s*a\\s*,\\s*l\\s*,\\s*j\\s*\\))")) {
            if (containsRegex(code, R"(cdq\\s*\\()") && containsRegex(code, R"(add\\s*\\()"))
                return {"O(n log^2 n)", "O(n)", "CDQ Divide & Conquer", "T(n) = 2T(n/2) + O(n log n)", "Master Theorem"};
            return {"O(n log n)", "O(n)", "Divide & Conquer / Segment Tree",
                    "T(n) = 2T(n/2) + O(n) => O(n log n)",
                    "Logarithmic interval halving with linear level work or canonical coverage pruning."};
        }

        // 9. Grid 2D BFS
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(queue\\s*<\\s*pair\\s*<\\s*int\\s*,\\s*int\\s*>\\s*>)") &&
            containsRegex(code, R"(vis\\s*\\[\\s*nx\\s*\\]\\s*\\[\\s*ny\\s*\\]\\s*=\\s*1)")) {
            return {"O(nm)", "O(nm)", "2D Grid BFS",
                    "Phi = |{(x,y) in [1..n]x[1..m] : vis[x][y] == 1}| <= nm",
                    "Each of the n*m grid cells is enqueued at most once with 4-neighbor O(1) expansion."};
        }

        // 10. Multi-parameter Knapsack O(nw) & Modulo DP O(nk) & LIS O(n log n)
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(for\\s*\\(\\s*int\\s+j\\s*=\\s*W\\s*;\\s*j\\s*>=\\s*w\\s*\\[\\s*i\\s*\\])")) {
            return {"O(nw)", "O(w)", "0/1 Knapsack DP", "N * W transitions", "Outer loop n items * inner loop capacity W."};
        }
        if (containsRegex(code, R"(for\\s*\\(\\s*int\\s+j\\s*=\\s*0\\s*;\\s*j\\s*<\\s*k\\s*;\\s*j\\s*\\+\\+\\s*\\))")) {
            return {"O(nk)", "O(nk)", "Modulo-k Parameterized DP", "N * K states", "Outer loop O(n) * inner residue loop O(k)."};
        }
        if (containsRegex(code, R"((lower_bound|upper_bound)\\s*\\()")) {
            return {"O(n log n)", "O(n)", "Binary Search Optimized Loop", "n * O(log n)", "O(log n) binary search inside O(n) loop."};
        }

        return {"O(n)", "O(n)", "Linear Scan", "Single pass", "Linear loop bound."};
    }
};
`;
